- **sql-correlated-update-from-another-tab-6d8330a76c6c89e4**: Shows how to update rows in one table using values from another with correlated subqueries and join-based updates. Use when backfilling a column from a lookup table, syncing denormalized fields, or fixing data where the new value depends on each row. Not for one-to-many updates where multiple source rows match (pick MERGE or dedupe first), or for bulk rewrites where a CTAS plus swap is cleaner. - **sql-cte-and-recursion**: Guides common table expressions for readable query decomposition (`WITH`) and `WITH RECURSIVE` for hierarchies, transitive closure, and series generation — always with a termination or cycle guard so recursion cannot run away. Auto-invokes when writing or editing `WITH`/`WITH RECURSIVE`, hierarchy/tree/graph traversal, transitive-closure or generate-series logic, deeply nested subqueries that want flattening, or on "infinite recursion" / "query runs forever" / "tree query" / "recursive query" requests. - **sql-cte-relation-does-not-exist-scope-fbc00c32db0a222a**: Fixes SQL CTE 'relation does not exist' scope errors.
